PDE - Chapter 212

1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any pde module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 390. The projective resolution has length at most 451 with Betti numbers bounded by 2.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to pde theory, we decompose the problem into 390 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 451. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(2/n). QED.
